   286→+
   287→+    Uses the OSC 11 escape sequence to query the terminal's background color.
   288→+    Returns "light", "dark", or "unknown" if detection fails.
   289→+    """
   290→+    if sys.platform == "win32":
   291→+        return "unknown"
   292→+
   293→+    if not sys.stdin.isatty() or not sys.stdout.isatty():
   294→+        return "unknown"
   295→+
   296→+    # Some terminals/environments don't support OSC queries
   297→+    term = os.environ.get("TERM", "")
   298→+    if term == "dumb" or os.environ.get("NO_COLOR"):
   299→+        return "unknown"
   300→+
   301→+    import select
   302→+    import termios
   303→+    import tty
   304→+
   305→+    fd = sys.stdin.fileno()
   306→+    try:
   307→+        old_attrs = termios.tcgetattr(fd)
   308→+    except termios.error:
   309→+        return "unknown"
   310→+
   311→+    try:
   312→+        tty.setraw(fd)
   313→+        # Send OSC 11 query: "what is the background color?"
   314→+        sys.stdout.write("\033]11;?\033\\")
   315→+        sys.stdout.flush()
   316→+
   317→+        # Wait for response with a short timeout
   318→+        if not select.select([sys.stdin], [], [], 0.15)[0]:
   319→+            return "unknown"
   320→+
   321→+        # Read response: expected format is \033]11;rgb:RRRR/GGGG/BBBB\033\\
   322→+        response = ""
   323→+        while select.select([sys.stdin], [], [], 0.05)[0]:
   324→+            ch = sys.stdin.read(1)
   325→+            response += ch
   326→+            if ch == "\\" or len(response) > 64:
   327→+                break
   328→+
   329→+        # Parse the response
   330→+        # Format: \033]11;rgb:RRRR/GGGG/BBBB\033\\ or \033]11;rgb:RR/GG/BB\a
   331→+        if "rgb:" in response:
   332→+            rgb_part = response.split("rgb:")[1].split("\033")[0].split("\a")[0]
   333→+            components = rgb_part.split("/")
   334→+            if len(components) == 3:
   335→+                # Take the first 2 hex digits of each component (they may be 2 or 4 digits)
   336→+                r = int(components[0][:2], 16)
   337→+                g = int(components[1][:2], 16)
   338→+                b = int(components[2][:2], 16)
   339→+                # Perceived luminance (ITU-R BT.601)
   340→+                luminance = 0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b
   341→+                return "light" if luminance > 128 else "dark"
   342→+
   343→+        return "unknown"
   344→+    except Exception:
   345→+        return "unknown"
   346→+    finally:
   347→+        try:
   348→+            termios.tcsetattr(fd, termios.TCSADRAIN, old_attrs)
   349→+        except Exception:
   350→+            pass

   384→+def save_config_value(key_path: str, value: any) -> bool:
   385→+    """Save a value to the active config file at the specified key path.
   386→+
   387→+    Respects the same lookup order as load_cli_config():
   388→+    1. ~/.hermes/config.yaml (user config - preferred, used if it exists)
   389→+    2. ./cli-config.yaml (project config - fallback)
   390→+
   391→+    Args:
   392→+        key_path: Dot-separated path like "agent.system_prompt"
   393→+        value: Value to save
   394→+
   395→+    Returns:
   396→+        True if successful, False otherwise
   397→+    """
   398→+    user_config_path = Path.home() / '.hermes' / 'config.yaml'
   399→+    project_config_path = Path(__file__).parent.parent / 'cli-config.yaml'
   400→+    config_path = user_config_path if user_config_path.exists() else project_config_path
   401→+
   402→+    try:
   403→+        config_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   404→+
   405→+        if config_path.exists():
   406→+            with open(config_path, 'r') as f:
   407→+                config = yaml.safe_load(f) or {}
   408→+        else:
   409→+            config = {}
   410→+
   411→+        keys = key_path.split('.')
   412→+        current = config
   413→+        for key in keys[:-1]:
   414→+            if key not in current or not isinstance(current[key], dict):
   415→+                current[key] = {}
   416→+            current = current[key]
   417→+        current[keys[-1]] = value
   418→+
   419→+        with open(config_path, 'w') as f:
   420→+            yaml.dump(config, f, default_flow_style=False, sort_keys=False)
   421→+
   422→+        try:
   423→+            os.chmod(config_path, 0o600)
   424→+        except (OSError, NotImplementedError):
   425→+            pass
   426→+
   427→+        return True
   428→+    except Exception as e:
   429→+        logger.error("Failed to save config: %s", e)
   430→+        return False
   431→+
   432→+
   433→ def save_config(config: Dict[str, Any]):
   434→     """Save configuration to ~/.hermes/config.yaml."""
   435→     from utils import atomic_yaml_write

   681→+def get_theme_mode() -> str:
   682→+    """Get the resolved theme mode ("light" or "dark").
   683→+
   684→+    If set to "auto", detects the terminal background on first call and caches it.
   685→+    Falls back to "dark" if detection fails.
   686→+
   687→+    When auto-detection succeeds, the result is persisted to
   688→+    ``display.detected_background`` in config.yaml so subsequent startups
   689→+    can skip the 200ms OSC 11 probe.  The cache is invalidated when the
   690→+    user runs ``/skin theme auto`` (which clears the key) or changes
   691→+    ``theme_mode`` explicitly.
   692→+    """
   693→+    global _resolved_theme_mode
   694→+    if _theme_mode in ("light", "dark"):
   695→+        return _theme_mode
   696→+    # Auto-detect
   697→+    if _resolved_theme_mode is None:
   698→+        try:
   699→+            from hermes_cli.colors import detect_terminal_background
   700→+            result = detect_terminal_background()
   701→+            _resolved_theme_mode = result if result != "unknown" else "dark"
   702→+        except Exception:
   703→+            _resolved_theme_mode = "dark"
   704→+        # Persist to config so next startup skips detection
   705→+        try:
   706→+            from hermes_cli.config import save_config_value
   707→+            save_config_value("display.detected_background", _resolved_theme_mode)
   708→+        except Exception:
   709→+            pass
   710→+    return _resolved_theme_mode
   711→+
   712→+
   713→+def get_theme_mode_setting() -> str:
   714→+    """Get the raw theme_mode setting ("auto", "light", or "dark")."""
   715→+    return _theme_mode
   716→+
   717→+
   718→+def set_theme_mode(mode: str) -> None:
   719→+    """Set the theme mode ("auto", "light", or "dark")."""
   720→+    global _theme_mode, _resolved_theme_mode
   721→+    if mode not in ("auto", "light", "dark"):
   722→+        mode = "auto"
   723→+    _theme_mode = mode
   724→+    _resolved_theme_mode = None  # Reset cache so auto re-detects
   725→+
   726→+
   727→ def get_active_skin() -> SkinConfig:
   728→     """Get the currently active skin config (cached)."""
   729→     global _active_skin
   730→@@ -618,13 +747,31 @@ def get_active_skin_name() -> str:
   731→

   733→ def init_skin_from_config(config: dict) -> None:
   734→-    """Initialize the active skin from CLI config at startup.
   735→+    """Initialize the active skin and theme mode from CLI config at startup.
   736→ 
   737→     Call this once during CLI init with the loaded config dict.
   738→+
   739→+    If ``theme_mode`` is ``auto`` and a previous detection result is cached
   740→+    in ``display.detected_background``, the cached value is used directly
   741→+    so we skip the OSC 11 probe (saves ~200ms on startup).
   742→     """
   743→+    global _resolved_theme_mode
   744→+
   745→     display = config.get("display", {})
   746→     skin_name = display.get("skin", "default")
   747→     if isinstance(skin_name, str) and skin_name.strip():
   748→         set_active_skin(skin_name.strip())
   749→     else:
   750→         set_active_skin("default")
   751→+
   752→+    theme_mode = display.get("theme_mode", "auto")
   753→+    if isinstance(theme_mode, str) and theme_mode.strip():
   754→+        set_theme_mode(theme_mode.strip())
   755→+    else:
   756→+        set_theme_mode("auto")
   757→+
   758→+    # Seed resolved mode from cached detection if available
   759→+    if _theme_mode == "auto":
   760→+        cached = display.get("detected_background")
   761→+        if cached in ("light", "dark"):
   762→+            _resolved_theme_mode = cached

1→"""Hermes CLI skin/theme engine.
     2→
     3→A data-driven skin system that lets users customize the CLI's visual appearance.
     4→Skins are defined as YAML files in ~/.hermes/skins/ or as built-in presets.
     5→No code changes are needed to add a new skin.
     6→
     7→SKIN YAML SCHEMA
     8→================
     9→
    10→All fields are optional. Missing values inherit from the ``default`` skin.
    11→
    12→.. code-block:: yaml
    13→
    14→    # Required: skin identity
    15→    name: mytheme                         # Unique skin name (lowercase, hyphens ok)
    16→    description: Short description        # Shown in /skin listing
    17→
    18→    # Colors: hex values for Rich markup (banner, UI, response box)
    19→    # These are the default (dark terminal) colors.
    20→    colors:
    21→      banner_border: "#CD7F32"            # Panel border color
    22→      banner_title: "#FFD700"             # Panel title text color
    23→      banner_accent: "#FFBF00"            # Section headers (Available Tools, etc.)
    24→      banner_dim: "#B8860B"               # Dim/muted text (separators, labels)
    25→      banner_text: "#FFF8DC"              # Body text (tool names, skill names)
    26→      ui_accent: "#FFBF00"               # General UI accent
    27→      ui_label: "#4dd0e1"                # UI labels
    28→      ui_ok: "#4caf50"                   # Success indicators
    29→      ui_error: "#ef5350"                # Error indicators
    30→      ui_warn: "#ffa726"                 # Warning indicators
    31→      prompt: "#FFF8DC"                  # Prompt text color
    32→      input_rule: "#CD7F32"              # Input area horizontal rule
    33→      response_border: "#FFD700"         # Response box border (ANSI)
    34→      session_label: "#DAA520"           # Session label color
    35→      session_border: "#8B8682"          # Session ID dim color
    36→
    37→    # Light-mode color overrides (optional). Only keys that need to change.
    38→    # When theme_mode is "light", these replace the corresponding colors above.
    39→    colors_light:
    40→      banner_title: "#B8860B"
    41→      prompt: "#5C4A00"
    42→
    43→    # Spinner: customize the animated spinner during API calls
    44→    spinner:
    45→      waiting_faces:                      # Faces shown while waiting for API
    46→        - "(⚔)"
    47→        - "(⛨)"
    48→      thinking_faces:                     # Faces shown during reasoning
    49→        - "(⌁)"
    50→        - "(<>)"
    51→      thinking_verbs:                     # Verbs for spinner messages
    52→        - "forging"
    53→        - "plotting"
    54→      wings:                              # Optional left/right spinner decorations
    55→        - ["⟪⚔", "⚔⟫"]                  # Each entry is [left, right] pair
    56→        - ["⟪▲", "▲⟫"]
    57→
    58→    # Branding: text strings used throughout the CLI
    59→    branding:
    60→      agent_name: "Hermes Agent"          # Banner title, status display
    61→      welcome: "Welcome message"          # Shown at CLI startup
    62→      goodbye: "Goodbye! ⚕"              # Shown on exit
    63→      response_label: " ⚕ Hermes "       # Response box header label
    64→      prompt_symbol: "❯ "                # Input prompt symbol
    65→      help_header: "(^_^)? Commands"      # /help header text
    66→
    67→    # Tool prefix: character for tool output lines (default: ┊)
    68→    tool_prefix: "┊"
    69→
    70→USAGE
    71→=====
    72→
    73→.. code-block:: python
    74→
    75→    from hermes_cli.skin_engine import get_active_skin, list_skins, set_active_skin
    76→
    77→    skin = get_active_skin()
    78→    print(skin.colors["banner_title"])    # "#FFD700"
    79→    print(skin.get_branding("agent_name"))  # "Hermes Agent"
    80→
    81→    set_active_skin("ares")               # Switch to built-in ares skin
    82→    set_active_skin("mytheme")            # Switch to user skin from ~/.hermes/skins/
    83→
    84→BUILT-IN SKINS
    85→==============
    86→
    87→- ``default`` — Classic Hermes gold/kawaii (the current look)
    88→- ``ares``    — Crimson/bronze war-god theme with custom spinner wings
    89→- ``mono``    — Clean grayscale monochrome
    90→- ``slate``   — Cool blue developer-focused theme
    91→
    92→USER SKINS
    93→==========
    94→
    95→Drop a YAML file in ``~/.hermes/skins/<name>.yaml`` following the schema above.
    96→Activate with ``/skin <name>`` in the CLI or ``display.skin: <name>`` in config.yaml.
    97→"""
    98→
    99→import logging
   100→import os
   101→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   102→from pathlib import Path
   103→from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
   104→
   105→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
   106→
   107→
   108→# =============================================================================
   109→# Skin data structure
   110→# =============================================================================
   111→
   112→@dataclass
   113→class SkinConfig:
   114→    """Complete skin configuration."""
   115→    name: str
   116→    description: str = ""
   117→    colors: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
   118→    colors_light: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
   119→    spinner: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
   120→    branding: Dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)
   121→    tool_prefix: str = "┊"
   122→    banner_logo: str = ""    # Rich-markup ASCII art logo (replaces HERMES_AGENT_LOGO)
   123→    banner_hero: str = ""    # Rich-markup hero art (replaces HERMES_CADUCEUS)
   124→
   125→    def get_color(self, key: str, fallback: str = "") -> str:
   126→        """Get a color value with fallback, respecting the active theme mode.
   127→
   128→        If the active theme mode is "light" and a light-mode override exists
   129→        for this key, return it. Otherwise return the default (dark) color.
   130→        """
   131→        mode = get_theme_mode()
   132→        if mode == "light" and key in self.colors_light:
   133→            return self.colors_light[key]
   134→        return self.colors.get(key, fallback)
   135→
   136→    def get_spinner_list(self, key: str) -> List[str]:
   137→        """Get a spinner list (faces, verbs, etc.)."""
   138→        return self.spinner.get(key, [])
   139→
   140→    def get_spinner_wings(self) -> List[Tuple[str, str]]:
   141→        """Get spinner wing pairs, or empty list if none."""
   142→        raw = self.spinner.get("wings", [])
   143→        result = []
   144→        for pair in raw:
   145→            if isinstance(pair, (list, tuple)) and len(pair) == 2:
   146→                result.append((str(pair[0]), str(pair[1])))
   147→        return result
   148→
   149→    def get_branding(self, key: str, fallback: str = "") -> str:
   150→        """Get a branding value with fallback."""
   151→        return self.branding.get(key, fallback)
   152→

   575→
   576→
   577→# =============================================================================
   578→# Skin loading and management
   579→# =============================================================================
   580→
   581→_active_skin: Optional[SkinConfig] = None
   582→_active_skin_name: str = "default"
   583→_theme_mode: str = "auto"  # "auto", "light", or "dark"
   584→_resolved_theme_mode: Optional[str] = None  # Cached result of auto-detection
   585→
   586→
   587→def _skins_dir() -> Path:
   588→    """User skins directory."""
   589→    home = Path(os.getenv("HERMES_HOME", Path.home() / ".hermes"))
   590→    return home / "skins"
   591→
   592→
   593→def _load_skin_from_yaml(path: Path) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
   594→    """Load a skin definition from a YAML file."""
   595→    try:
   596→        import yaml
   597→        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   598→            data = yaml.safe_load(f)
   599→        if isinstance(data, dict) and "name" in data:
   600→            return data
   601→    except Exception as e:
   602→        logger.debug("Failed to load skin from %s: %s", path, e)
   603→    return None
   604→
   605→
   606→def _build_skin_config(data: Dict[str, Any]) -> SkinConfig:
   607→    """Build a SkinConfig from a raw dict (built-in or loaded from YAML)."""
   608→    # Start with default values as base for missing keys
   609→    default = _BUILTIN_SKINS["default"]
   610→    colors = dict(default.get("colors", {}))
   611→    colors.update(data.get("colors", {}))
   612→    colors_light = dict(default.get("colors_light", {}))
   613→    colors_light.update(data.get("colors_light", {}))
   614→    spinner = dict(default.get("spinner", {}))
   615→    spinner.update(data.get("spinner", {}))
   616→    branding = dict(default.get("branding", {}))
   617→    branding.update(data.get("branding", {}))
   618→
   619→    return SkinConfig(
   620→        name=data.get("name", "unknown"),
   621→        description=data.get("description", ""),
   622→        colors=colors,
   623→        colors_light=colors_light,
   624→        spinner=spinner,
   625→        branding=branding,
   626→        tool_prefix=data.get("tool_prefix", default.get("tool_prefix", "┊")),
   627→        banner_logo=data.get("banner_logo", ""),
   628→        banner_hero=data.get("banner_hero", ""),
   629→    )
   630→
   631→
   632→def list_skins() -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
   633→    """List all available skins (built-in + user-installed).
   634→
   635→    Returns list of {"name": ..., "description": ..., "source": "builtin"|"user"}.
   636→    """
   637→    result = []
   638→    for name, data in _BUILTIN_SKINS.items():
   639→        result.append({
   640→            "name": name,
   641→            "description": data.get("description", ""),
   642→            "source": "builtin",
   643→        })
   644→
   645→    skins_path = _skins_dir()
   646→    if skins_path.is_dir():
   647→        for f in sorted(skins_path.glob("*.yaml")):
   648→            data = _load_skin_from_yaml(f)
   649→            if data:
   650→                skin_name = data.get("name", f.stem)
   651→                # Skip if it shadows a built-in
   652→                if any(s["name"] == skin_name for s in result):
   653→                    continue
   654→                result.append({
   655→                    "name": skin_name,
   656→                    "description": data.get("description", ""),
   657→                    "source": "user",
   658→                })
   659→
   660→    return result
   661→
   662→
   663→def load_skin(name: str) -> SkinConfig:
   664→    """Load a skin by name. Checks user skins first, then built-in."""
   665→    # Check user skins directory
   666→    skins_path = _skins_dir()
   667→    user_file = skins_path / f"{name}.yaml"
   668→    if user_file.is_file():
   669→        data = _load_skin_from_yaml(user_file)
   670→        if data:
   671→            return _build_skin_config(data)
   672→
   673→    # Check built-in skins
   674→    if name in _BUILTIN_SKINS:
   675→        return _build_skin_config(_BUILTIN_SKINS[name])
   676→
   677→    # Fallback to default
   678→    logger.warning("Skin '%s' not found, using default", name)
   679→    return _build_skin_config(_BUILTIN_SKINS["default"])
   680→
   681→
   682→def get_theme_mode() -> str:
   683→    """Get the resolved theme mode ("light" or "dark").
   684→
   685→    If set to "auto", detects the terminal background on first call and caches it.
   686→    Falls back to "dark" if detection fails.
   687→
   688→    When auto-detection succeeds, the result is persisted to
   689→    ``display.detected_background`` in config.yaml so subsequent startups
   690→    can skip the 200ms OSC 11 probe.  The cache is invalidated when the
   691→    user runs ``/skin theme auto`` (which clears the key) or changes
   692→    ``theme_mode`` explicitly.
   693→    """
   694→    global _resolved_theme_mode
   695→    if _theme_mode in ("light", "dark"):
   696→        return _theme_mode
   697→    # Auto-detect
   698→    if _resolved_theme_mode is None:
   699→        try:
   700→            from hermes_cli.colors import detect_terminal_background
   701→            result = detect_terminal_background()
   702→            _resolved_theme_mode = result if result != "unknown" else "dark"
   703→        except Exception:
   704→            _resolved_theme_mode = "dark"
   705→        # Persist to config so next startup skips detection
   706→        try:
   707→            from hermes_cli.config import save_config_value
   708→            save_config_value("display.detected_background", _resolved_theme_mode)
   709→        except Exception:
   710→            pass
   711→    return _resolved_theme_mode
   712→
   713→
   714→def get_theme_mode_setting() -> str:
   715→    """Get the raw theme_mode setting ("auto", "light", or "dark")."""
   716→    return _theme_mode
   717→
   718→
   719→def set_theme_mode(mode: str) -> None:
   720→    """Set the theme mode ("auto", "light", or "dark")."""
   721→    global _theme_mode, _resolved_theme_mode
   722→    if mode not in ("auto", "light", "dark"):
   723→        mode = "auto"
   724→    _theme_mode = mode
   725→    _resolved_theme_mode = None  # Reset cache so auto re-detects
   726→
   727→
   728→def get_active_skin() -> SkinConfig:
   729→    """Get the currently active skin config (cached)."""
   730→    global _active_skin
   731→    if _active_skin is None:
   732→        _active_skin = load_skin(_active_skin_name)
   733→    return _active_skin
   734→
   735→
   736→def set_active_skin(name: str) -> SkinConfig:
   737→    """Switch the active skin. Returns the new SkinConfig."""
   738→    global _active_skin, _active_skin_name
   739→    _active_skin_name = name
   740→    _active_skin = load_skin(name)
   741→    return _active_skin
   742→
   743→
   744→def get_active_skin_name() -> str:
   745→    """Get the name of the currently active skin."""
   746→    return _active_skin_name
   747→
   748→
   749→def init_skin_from_config(config: dict) -> None:
   750→    """Initialize the active skin and theme mode from CLI config at startup.
   751→
   752→    Call this once during CLI init with the loaded config dict.
   753→
   754→    If ``theme_mode`` is ``auto`` and a previous detection result is cached
   755→    in ``display.detected_background``, the cached value is used directly
   756→    so we skip the OSC 11 probe (saves ~200ms on startup).
   757→    """
   758→    global _resolved_theme_mode
   759→
   760→    display = config.get("display", {})
   761→    skin_name = display.get("skin", "default")
   762→    if isinstance(skin_name, str) and skin_name.strip():
   763→        set_active_skin(skin_name.strip())
   764→    else:
   765→        set_active_skin("default")
   766→
   767→    theme_mode = display.get("theme_mode", "auto")
   768→    if isinstance(theme_mode, str) and theme_mode.strip():
   769→        set_theme_mode(theme_mode.strip())
   770→    else:
   771→        set_theme_mode("auto")
   772→
   773→    # Seed resolved mode from cached detection if available
   774→    if _theme_mode == "auto":
   775→        cached = display.get("detected_background")
   776→        if cached in ("light", "dark"):
   777→            _resolved_theme_mode = cached
   778→
```

1→"""Shared ANSI color utilities for Hermes CLI modules."""
     2→
     3→import os
     4→import sys
     5→
     6→
     7→class Colors:
     8→    RESET = "\033[0m"
     9→    BOLD = "\033[1m"
    10→    DIM = "\033[2m"
    11→    RED = "\033[31m"
    12→    GREEN = "\033[32m"
    13→    YELLOW = "\033[33m"
    14→    BLUE = "\033[34m"
    15→    MAGENTA = "\033[35m"
    16→    CYAN = "\033[36m"
    17→
    18→
    19→def color(text: str, *codes) -> str:
    20→    """Apply color codes to text (only when output is a TTY)."""
    21→    if not sys.stdout.isatty():
    22→        return text
    23→    return "".join(codes) + text + Colors.RESET
    24→
    25→
    26→def detect_terminal_background() -> str:
    27→    """Detect whether the terminal has a light or dark background.
    28→
    29→    Uses the OSC 11 escape sequence to query the terminal's background color.
    30→    Returns "light", "dark", or "unknown" if detection fails.
    31→    """
    32→    if sys.platform == "win32":
    33→        return "unknown"
    34→
    35→    if not sys.stdin.isatty() or not sys.stdout.isatty():
    36→        return "unknown"
    37→
    38→    # Some terminals/environments don't support OSC queries
    39→    term = os.environ.get("TERM", "")
    40→    if term == "dumb" or os.environ.get("NO_COLOR"):
    41→        return "unknown"
    42→
    43→    import select
    44→    import termios
    45→    import tty
    46→
    47→    fd = sys.stdin.fileno()
    48→    try:
    49→        old_attrs = termios.tcgetattr(fd)
    50→    except termios.error:
    51→        return "unknown"
    52→
    53→    try:
    54→        tty.setraw(fd)
    55→        # Send OSC 11 query: "what is the background color?"
    56→        sys.stdout.write("\033]11;?\033\\")
    57→        sys.stdout.flush()
    58→
    59→        # Wait for response with a short timeout
    60→        if not select.select([sys.stdin], [], [], 0.15)[0]:
    61→            return "unknown"
    62→
    63→        # Read response: expected format is \033]11;rgb:RRRR/GGGG/BBBB\033\\
    64→        response = ""
    65→        while select.select([sys.stdin], [], [], 0.05)[0]:
    66→            ch = sys.stdin.read(1)
    67→            response += ch
    68→            if ch == "\\" or len(response) > 64:
    69→                break
    70→
    71→        # Parse the response
    72→        # Format: \033]11;rgb:RRRR/GGGG/BBBB\033\\ or \033]11;rgb:RR/GG/BB\a
    73→        if "rgb:" in response:
    74→            rgb_part = response.split("rgb:")[1].split("\033")[0].split("\a")[0]
    75→            components = rgb_part.split("/")
    76→            if len(components) == 3:
    77→                # Take the first 2 hex digits of each component (they may be 2 or 4 digits)
    78→                r = int(components[0][:2], 16)
    79→                g = int(components[1][:2], 16)
    80→                b = int(components[2][:2], 16)
    81→                # Perceived luminance (ITU-R BT.601)
    82→                luminance = 0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b
    83→                return "light" if luminance > 128 else "dark"
    84→
    85→        return "unknown"
    86→    except Exception:
    87→        return "unknown"
    88→    finally:
    89→        try:
    90→            termios.tcsetattr(fd, termios.TCSADRAIN, old_attrs)
    91→        except Exception:
    92→            pass
    93→
```

   172→# =========================================================================
   173→# KawaiiSpinner
   174→# =========================================================================
   175→
   176→class KawaiiSpinner:
   177→    """Animated spinner with kawaii faces for CLI feedback during tool execution."""
   178→
   179→    SPINNERS = {
   180→        'dots': ['⠋', '⠙', '⠹', '⠸', '⠼', '⠴', '⠦', '⠧', '⠇', '⠏'],
   181→        'bounce': ['⠁', '⠂', '⠄', '⡀', '⢀', '⠠', '⠐', '⠈'],
   182→        'grow': ['▁', '▂', '▃', '▄', '▅', '▆', '▇', '█', '▇', '▆', '▅', '▄', '▃', '▂'],
   183→        'arrows': ['←', '↖', '↑', '↗', '→', '↘', '↓', '↙'],
   184→        'star': ['✶', '✷', '✸', '✹', '✺', '✹', '✸', '✷'],
   185→        'moon': ['🌑', '🌒', '🌓', '🌔', '🌕', '🌖', '🌗', '🌘'],
   186→        'pulse': ['◜', '◠', '◝', '◞', '◡', '◟'],
   187→        'brain': ['🧠', '💭', '💡', '✨', '💫', '🌟', '💡', '💭'],
   188→        'sparkle': ['⁺', '˚', '*', '✧', '✦', '✧', '*', '˚'],
   189→    }
   190→
   191→    KAWAII_WAITING = [
   192→        "(｡◕‿◕｡)", "(◕‿◕✿)", "٩(◕‿◕｡)۶", "(✿◠‿◠)", "( ˘▽˘)っ",
   193→        "♪(´ε` )", "(◕ᴗ◕✿)", "ヾ(＾∇＾)", "(≧◡≦)", "(★ω★)",
   194→    ]
   195→
   196→    KAWAII_THINKING = [
   197→        "(｡•́︿•̀｡)", "(◔_◔)", "(¬‿¬)", "( •_•)>⌐■-■", "(⌐■_■)",
   198→        "(´･_･`)", "◉_◉", "(°ロ°)", "( ˘⌣˘)♡", "ヽ(>∀<☆)☆",
   199→        "٩(๑❛ᴗ❛๑)۶", "(⊙_⊙)", "(¬_¬)", "( ͡° ͜ʖ ͡°)", "ಠ_ಠ",
   200→    ]
   201→
   202→    THINKING_VERBS = [
   203→        "pondering", "contemplating", "musing", "cogitating", "ruminating",
   204→        "deliberating", "mulling", "reflecting", "processing", "reasoning",
   205→        "analyzing", "computing", "synthesizing", "formulating", "brainstorming",
   206→    ]
   207→
   208→    def __init__(self, message: str = "", spinner_type: str = 'dots'):
   209→        self.message = message
   210→        self.spinner_frames = self.SPINNERS.get(spinner_type, self.SPINNERS['dots'])
   211→        self.running = False
   212→        self.thread = None
   213→        self.frame_idx = 0
   214→        self.start_time = None
   215→        self.last_line_len = 0
   216→        self._last_flush_time = 0.0  # Rate-limit flushes for patch_stdout compat
   217→        # Capture stdout NOW, before any redirect_stdout(devnull) from
   218→        # child agents can replace sys.stdout with a black hole.
   219→        self._out = sys.stdout
   220→
   221→    def _write(self, text: str, end: str = '\n', flush: bool = False):
   222→        """Write to the stdout captured at spinner creation time."""
   223→        try:
   224→            self._out.write(text + end)
   225→            if flush:
   226→                self._out.flush()
   227→        except (ValueError, OSError):
   228→            pass
   229→
   230→    def _animate(self):
   231→        # Cache skin wings at start (avoid per-frame imports)
   232→        skin = _get_skin()
   233→        wings = skin.get_spinner_wings() if skin else []
   234→
   235→        while self.running:
   236→            if os.getenv("HERMES_SPINNER_PAUSE"):
   237→                time.sleep(0.1)
   238→                continue
   239→            frame = self.spinner_frames[self.frame_idx % len(self.spinner_frames)]
   240→            elapsed = time.time() - self.start_time
   241→            if wings:
   242→                left, right = wings[self.frame_idx % len(wings)]
   243→                line = f"  {left} {frame} {self.message} {right} ({elapsed:.1f}s)"
   244→            else:
   245→                line = f"  {frame} {self.message} ({elapsed:.1f}s)"
   246→            pad = max(self.last_line_len - len(line), 0)
   247→            # Rate-limit flush() calls to avoid spinner spam under
   248→            # prompt_toolkit's patch_stdout.  Each flush() pushes a queue
   249→            # item that may trigger a separate run_in_terminal() call; if
   250→            # items are processed one-at-a-time the \r overwrite is lost
   251→            # and every frame appears on its own line.  By flushing at
   252→            # most every 0.4s we guarantee multiple \r-frames are batched
   253→            # into a single write, so the terminal collapses them correctly.
   254→            now = time.time()
   255→            should_flush = (now - self._last_flush_time) >= 0.4
   256→            self._write(f"\r{line}{' ' * pad}", end='', flush=should_flush)
   257→            if should_flush:
   258→                self._last_flush_time = now
   259→            self.last_line_len = len(line)
   260→            self.frame_idx += 1
   261→            time.sleep(0.12)
   262→
   263→    def start(self):
   264→        if self.running:
   265→            return
   266→        self.running = True
   267→        self.start_time = time.time()
   268→        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._animate, daemon=True)
   269→        self.thread.start()
   270→
   271→    def update_text(self, new_message: str):
   272→        self.message = new_message
   273→
   274→    def print_above(self, text: str):
   275→        """Print a line above the spinner without disrupting animation.
   276→
   277→        Clears the current spinner line, prints the text, and lets the
   278→        next animation tick redraw the spinner on the line below.
   279→        Thread-safe: uses the captured stdout reference (self._out).
   280→        Works inside redirect_stdout(devnull) because _write bypasses
   281→        sys.stdout and writes to the stdout captured at spinner creation.
   282→        """
   283→        if not self.running:
   284→            self._write(f"  {text}", flush=True)
   285→            return
   286→        # Clear spinner line with spaces (not \033[K) to avoid garbled escape
   287→        # codes when prompt_toolkit's patch_stdout is active — same approach
   288→        # as stop(). Then print text; spinner redraws on next tick.
   289→        blanks = ' ' * max(self.last_line_len + 5, 40)
   290→        self._write(f"\r{blanks}\r  {text}", flush=True)
   291→
   292→    def stop(self, final_message: str = None):
   293→        self.running = False
   294→        if self.thread:
   295→            self.thread.join(timeout=0.5)
   296→        # Clear the spinner line with spaces instead of \033[K to avoid
   297→        # garbled escape codes when prompt_toolkit's patch_stdout is active.
   298→        blanks = ' ' * max(self.last_line_len + 5, 40)
   299→        self._write(f"\r{blanks}\r", end='', flush=True)
   300→        if final_message:
   301→            self._write(f"  {final_message}", flush=True)
   302→
   303→    def __enter__(self):
   304→        self.start()
   305→        return self
   306→
   307→    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_val, exc_tb):
   308→        self.stop()
   309→        return False

   352→# =========================================================================
   353→# Cute tool message (completion line that replaces the spinner)
   354→# =========================================================================
   355→
   356→def _detect_tool_failure(tool_name: str, result: str | None) -> tuple[bool, str]:
   357→    """Inspect a tool result string for signs of failure.
   358→
   359→    Returns ``(is_failure, suffix)`` where *suffix* is an informational tag
   360→    like ``" [exit 1]"`` for terminal failures, or ``" [error]"`` for generic
   361→    failures.  On success, returns ``(False, "")``.
   362→    """
   363→    if result is None:
   364→        return False, ""
   365→
   366→    if tool_name == "terminal":
   367→        try:
   368→            data = json.loads(result)
   369→            exit_code = data.get("exit_code")
   370→            if exit_code is not None and exit_code != 0:
   371→                return True, f" [exit {exit_code}]"
   372→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   373→            logger.debug("Could not parse terminal result as JSON for exit code check")
   374→        return False, ""
   375→
   376→    # Memory-specific: distinguish "full" from real errors
   377→    if tool_name == "memory":
   378→        try:
   379→            data = json.loads(result)
   380→            if data.get("success") is False and "exceed the limit" in data.get("error", ""):
   381→                return True, " [full]"
   382→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   383→            logger.debug("Could not parse memory result as JSON for capacity check")
   384→
   385→    # Generic heuristic for non-terminal tools
   386→    lower = result[:500].lower()
   387→    if '"error"' in lower or '"failed"' in lower or result.startswith("Error"):
   388→        return True, " [error]"
   389→
   390→    return False, ""
   391→
   392→
   393→def get_cute_tool_message(
   394→    tool_name: str, args: dict, duration: float, result: str | None = None,
   395→) -> str:
   396→    """Generate a formatted tool completion line for CLI quiet mode.
   397→
   398→    Format: ``| {emoji} {verb:9} {detail}  {duration}``
   399→
   400→    When *result* is provided the line is checked for failure indicators.
   401→    Failed tool calls get a red prefix and an informational suffix.
   402→    """
   403→    dur = f"{duration:.1f}s"
   404→    is_failure, failure_suffix = _detect_tool_failure(tool_name, result)
   405→    skin_prefix = get_skin_tool_prefix()
   406→
   407→    def _trunc(s, n=40):
   408→        s = str(s)
   409→        return (s[:n-3] + "...") if len(s) > n else s
   410→
   411→    def _path(p, n=35):
   412→        p = str(p)
   413→        return ("..." + p[-(n-3):]) if len(p) > n else p
   414→
   415→    def _wrap(line: str) -> str:
   416→        """Apply skin tool prefix and failure suffix."""
   417→        if skin_prefix != "┊":
   418→            line = line.replace("┊", skin_prefix, 1)
   419→        if not is_failure:
   420→            return line
   421→        return f"{line}{failure_suffix}"
   422→
   423→    if tool_name == "web_search":
   424→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🔍 search    {_trunc(args.get('query', ''), 42)}  {dur}")
   425→    if tool_name == "web_extract":
   426→        urls = args.get("urls", [])
   427→        if urls:
   428→            url = urls[0] if isinstance(urls, list) else str(urls)
   429→            domain = url.replace("https://", "").replace("http://", "").split("/")[0]
   430→            extra = f" +{len(urls)-1}" if len(urls) > 1 else ""
   431→            return _wrap(f"┊ 📄 fetch     {_trunc(domain, 35)}{extra}  {dur}")
   432→        return _wrap(f"┊ 📄 fetch     pages  {dur}")
   433→    if tool_name == "web_crawl":
   434→        url = args.get("url", "")
   435→        domain = url.replace("https://", "").replace("http://", "").split("/")[0]
   436→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🕸️  crawl     {_trunc(domain, 35)}  {dur}")
   437→    if tool_name == "terminal":
   438→        return _wrap(f"┊ 💻 $         {_trunc(args.get('command', ''), 42)}  {dur}")
   439→    if tool_name == "process":
   440→        action = args.get("action", "?")
   441→        sid = args.get("session_id", "")[:12]
   442→        labels = {"list": "ls processes", "poll": f"poll {sid}", "log": f"log {sid}",
   443→                  "wait": f"wait {sid}", "kill": f"kill {sid}", "write": f"write {sid}", "submit": f"submit {sid}"}
   444→        return _wrap(f"┊ ⚙️  proc      {labels.get(action, f'{action} {sid}')}  {dur}")
   445→    if tool_name == "read_file":
   446→        return _wrap(f"┊ 📖 read      {_path(args.get('path', ''))}  {dur}")
   447→    if tool_name == "write_file":
   448→        return _wrap(f"┊ ✍️  write     {_path(args.get('path', ''))}  {dur}")
   449→    if tool_name == "patch":
   450→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🔧 patch     {_path(args.get('path', ''))}  {dur}")
   451→    if tool_name == "search_files":
   452→        pattern = _trunc(args.get("pattern", ""), 35)
   453→        target = args.get("target", "content")
   454→        verb = "find" if target == "files" else "grep"
   455→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🔎 {verb:9} {pattern}  {dur}")
   456→    if tool_name == "browser_navigate":
   457→        url = args.get("url", "")
   458→        domain = url.replace("https://", "").replace("http://", "").split("/")[0]
   459→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🌐 navigate  {_trunc(domain, 35)}  {dur}")
   460→    if tool_name == "browser_snapshot":
   461→        mode = "full" if args.get("full") else "compact"
   462→        return _wrap(f"┊ 📸 snapshot  {mode}  {dur}")
   463→    if tool_name == "browser_click":
   464→        return _wrap(f"┊ 👆 click     {args.get('ref', '?')}  {dur}")
   465→    if tool_name == "browser_type":
   466→        return _wrap(f"┊ ⌨️  type      \"{_trunc(args.get('text', ''), 30)}\"  {dur}")
   467→    if tool_name == "browser_scroll":
   468→        d = args.get("direction", "down")
   469→        arrow = {"down": "↓", "up": "↑", "right": "→", "left": "←"}.get(d, "↓")
   470→        return _wrap(f"┊ {arrow}  scroll    {d}  {dur}")
   471→    if tool_name == "browser_back":
   472→        return _wrap(f"┊ ◀️  back      {dur}")
   473→    if tool_name == "browser_press":
   474→        return _wrap(f"┊ ⌨️  press     {args.get('key', '?')}  {dur}")
   475→    if tool_name == "browser_close":
   476→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🚪 close     browser  {dur}")
   477→    if tool_name == "browser_get_images":
   478→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🖼️  images    extracting  {dur}")
   479→    if tool_name == "browser_vision":
   480→        return _wrap(f"┊ 👁️  vision    analyzing page  {dur}")
   481→    if tool_name == "todo":
   482→        todos_arg = args.get("todos")
   483→        merge = args.get("merge", False)
   484→        if todos_arg is None:
   485→            return _wrap(f"┊ 📋 plan      reading tasks  {dur}")
   486→        elif merge:
   487→            return _wrap(f"┊ 📋 plan      update {len(todos_arg)} task(s)  {dur}")
   488→        else:
   489→            return _wrap(f"┊ 📋 plan      {len(todos_arg)} task(s)  {dur}")
   490→    if tool_name == "session_search":
   491→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🔍 recall    \"{_trunc(args.get('query', ''), 35)}\"  {dur}")
   492→    if tool_name == "memory":
   493→        action = args.get("action", "?")
   494→        target = args.get("target", "")
   495→        if action == "add":
   496→            return _wrap(f"┊ 🧠 memory    +{target}: \"{_trunc(args.get('content', ''), 30)}\"  {dur}")
   497→        elif action == "replace":
   498→            return _wrap(f"┊ 🧠 memory    ~{target}: \"{_trunc(args.get('old_text', ''), 20)}\"  {dur}")
   499→        elif action == "remove":
   500→            return _wrap(f"┊ 🧠 memory    -{target}: \"{_trunc(args.get('old_text', ''), 20)}\"  {dur}")
   501→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🧠 memory    {action}  {dur}")
   502→    if tool_name == "skills_list":
   503→        return _wrap(f"┊ 📚 skills    list {args.get('category', 'all')}  {dur}")
   504→    if tool_name == "skill_view":
   505→        return _wrap(f"┊ 📚 skill     {_trunc(args.get('name', ''), 30)}  {dur}")
   506→    if tool_name == "image_generate":
   507→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🎨 create    {_trunc(args.get('prompt', ''), 35)}  {dur}")
   508→    if tool_name == "text_to_speech":
   509→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🔊 speak     {_trunc(args.get('text', ''), 30)}  {dur}")
   510→    if tool_name == "vision_analyze":
   511→        return _wrap(f"┊ 👁️  vision    {_trunc(args.get('question', ''), 30)}  {dur}")
   512→    if tool_name == "mixture_of_agents":
   513→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🧠 reason    {_trunc(args.get('user_prompt', ''), 30)}  {dur}")
   514→    if tool_name == "send_message":
   515→        return _wrap(f"┊ 📨 send      {args.get('target', '?')}: \"{_trunc(args.get('message', ''), 25)}\"  {dur}")
   516→    if tool_name == "schedule_cronjob":
   517→        return _wrap(f"┊ ⏰ schedule  {_trunc(args.get('name', args.get('prompt', 'task')), 30)}  {dur}")
   518→    if tool_name == "list_cronjobs":
   519→        return _wrap(f"┊ ⏰ jobs      listing  {dur}")
   520→    if tool_name == "remove_cronjob":
   521→        return _wrap(f"┊ ⏰ remove    job {args.get('job_id', '?')}  {dur}")
   522→    if tool_name.startswith("rl_"):
   523→        rl = {
   524→            "rl_list_environments": "list envs", "rl_select_environment": f"select {args.get('name', '')}",
   525→            "rl_get_current_config": "get config", "rl_edit_config": f"set {args.get('field', '?')}",
   526→            "rl_start_training": "start training", "rl_check_status": f"status {args.get('run_id', '?')[:12]}",
   527→            "rl_stop_training": f"stop {args.get('run_id', '?')[:12]}", "rl_get_results": f"results {args.get('run_id', '?')[:12]}",
   528→            "rl_list_runs": "list runs", "rl_test_inference": "test inference",
   529→        }
   530→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🧪 rl        {rl.get(tool_name, tool_name.replace('rl_', ''))}  {dur}")
   531→    if tool_name == "execute_code":
   532→        code = args.get("code", "")
   533→        first_line = code.strip().split("\n")[0] if code.strip() else ""
   534→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🐍 exec      {_trunc(first_line, 35)}  {dur}")
   535→    if tool_name == "delegate_task":
   536→        tasks = args.get("tasks")
   537→        if tasks and isinstance(tasks, list):
   538→            return _wrap(f"┊ 🔀 delegate  {len(tasks)} parallel tasks  {dur}")
   539→        return _wrap(f"┊ 🔀 delegate  {_trunc(args.get('goal', ''), 35)}  {dur}")
   540→
   541→    preview = build_tool_preview(tool_name, args) or ""
   542→    return _wrap(f"┊ ⚡ {tool_name[:9]:9} {_trunc(preview, 35)}  {dur}")
   543→
   544→
   545→# =========================================================================
   546→# Honcho session line (one-liner with clickable OSC 8 hyperlink)
   547→# =========================================================================
   548→
   549→_DIM = "\033[2m"
   550→_SKY_BLUE = "\033[38;5;117m"
   551→_ANSI_RESET = "\033[0m"
   552→
   553→
   554→def honcho_session_url(workspace: str, session_name: str) -> str:
   555→    """Build a Honcho app URL for a session."""
   556→    from urllib.parse import quote
   557→    return (
   558→        f"https://app.honcho.dev/explore"
   559→        f"?workspace={quote(workspace, safe='')}"
   560→        f"&view=sessions"
   561→        f"&session={quote(session_name, safe='')}"
   562→    )
   563→
   564→
   565→def _osc8_link(url: str, text: str) -> str:
   566→    """OSC 8 terminal hyperlink (clickable in iTerm2, Ghostty, WezTerm, etc.)."""
   567→    return f"\033]8;;{url}\033\\{text}\033]8;;\033\\"
   568→
   569→
   570→def honcho_session_line(workspace: str, session_name: str) -> str:
   571→    """One-line session indicator: `Honcho session: <clickable name>`."""
   572→    url = honcho_session_url(workspace, session_name)
   573→    linked_name = _osc8_link(url, f"{_SKY_BLUE}{session_name}{_ANSI_RESET}")
   574→    return f"{_DIM}Honcho session:{_ANSI_RESET} {linked_name}"
   575→
   576→
   577→def write_tty(text: str) -> None:
   578→    """Write directly to /dev/tty, bypassing stdout capture."""
   579→    try:
   580→        fd = os.open("/dev/tty", os.O_WRONLY)
   581→        os.write(fd, text.encode("utf-8"))
   582→        os.close(fd)
   583→    except OSError:
   584→        sys.stdout.write(text)
   585→        sys.stdout.flush()
   586→

1→"""Hermes CLI skin/theme engine.
     2→
     3→A data-driven skin system that lets users customize the CLI's visual appearance.
     4→Skins are defined as YAML files in ~/.hermes/skins/ or as built-in presets.
     5→No code changes are needed to add a new skin.
     6→
     7→SKIN YAML SCHEMA
     8→================
     9→
    10→All fields are optional. Missing values inherit from the ``default`` skin.
    11→
    12→.. code-block:: yaml
    13→
    14→    # Required: skin identity
    15→    name: mytheme                         # Unique skin name (lowercase, hyphens ok)
    16→    description: Short description        # Shown in /skin listing
    17→
    18→    # Colors: hex values for Rich markup (banner, UI, response box)
    19→    # These are the default (dark terminal) colors.
    20→    colors:
    21→      banner_border: "#CD7F32"            # Panel border color
    22→      banner_title: "#FFD700"             # Panel title text color
    23→      banner_accent: "#FFBF00"            # Section headers (Available Tools, etc.)
    24→      banner_dim: "#B8860B"               # Dim/muted text (separators, labels)
    25→      banner_text: "#FFF8DC"              # Body text (tool names, skill names)
    26→      ui_accent: "#FFBF00"               # General UI accent
    27→      ui_label: "#4dd0e1"                # UI labels
    28→      ui_ok: "#4caf50"                   # Success indicators
    29→      ui_error: "#ef5350"                # Error indicators
    30→      ui_warn: "#ffa726"                 # Warning indicators
    31→      prompt: "#FFF8DC"                  # Prompt text color
    32→      input_rule: "#CD7F32"              # Input area horizontal rule
    33→      response_border: "#FFD700"         # Response box border (ANSI)
    34→      session_label: "#DAA520"           # Session label color
    35→      session_border: "#8B8682"          # Session ID dim color
    36→
    37→    # Light-mode color overrides (optional). Only keys that need to change.
    38→    # When theme_mode is "light", these replace the corresponding colors above.
    39→    colors_light:
    40→      banner_title: "#B8860B"
    41→      prompt: "#5C4A00"
    42→
    43→    # Spinner: customize the animated spinner during API calls

    45→      waiting_faces:                      # Faces shown while waiting for API
    46→        - "(⚔)"
    47→        - "(⛨)"
    48→      thinking_faces:                     # Faces shown during reasoning
    49→        - "(⌁)"

    61→      welcome: "Welcome message"          # Shown at CLI startup
    62→      goodbye: "Goodbye! ⚕"              # Shown on exit
    63→      response_label: " ⚕ Hermes "       # Response box header label

    68→    tool_prefix: "┊"

    97→"""

   126→        """Get a color value with fallback, respecting the active theme mode.

   565→[#C75B1D]⠀⣼⠟⠀⢀⣾⠟⠁⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠈⠻⣷⡀⠀⠻⣧⠀[/]
   566→[#C75B1D]⢸⡟⠀⠀⣿⡟⠀⠀⠀🔥⠀⠀⠀⠀⢻⣿⠀⠀⢻⡇[/]
   567→[#7A3511]⠀⠻⣦⡀⠘⢿⣧⡀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⢀⣼⡿⠃⢀⣴⠟⠀[/]
   568→[#7A3511]⠀⠀⠈⠻⣦⣀⠙⢿⣷⣤⣤⣤⣾⡿⠋⣀⣴⠟⠁⠀⠀[/]
   569→[#C75B1D]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠈⠙⠛⠶⠤⠭⠭⠤⠶⠛⠋⠁⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]
   570→[#F29C38]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⣰⡿⢿⣆⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]
   571→[#F29C38]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⣼⡟⠀⠀⢻⣧⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]
   572→[dim #7A3511]⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀tail flame lit⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀⠀[/]""",
   573→    },
   574→}
   575→
   576→
   577→# =============================================================================
   578→# Skin loading and management
   579→# =============================================================================
   580→
   581→_active_skin: Optional[SkinConfig] = None
   582→_active_skin_name: str = "default"
   583→_theme_mode: str = "auto"  # "auto", "light", or "dark"
   584→_resolved_theme_mode: Optional[str] = None  # Cached result of auto-detection
   585→
   586→
   587→def _skins_dir() -> Path:
   588→    """User skins directory."""
   589→    home = Path(os.getenv("HERMES_HOME", Path.home() / ".hermes"))
   590→    return home / "skins"
   591→
   592→
   593→def _load_skin_from_yaml(path: Path) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
   594→    """Load a skin definition from a YAML file."""
   595→    try:
   596→        import yaml
   597→        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   598→            data = yaml.safe_load(f)
   599→        if isinstance(data, dict) and "name" in data:
   600→            return data
   601→    except Exception as e:
   602→        logger.debug("Failed to load skin from %s: %s", path, e)
   603→    return None
   604→
   605→
   606→def _build_skin_config(data: Dict[str, Any]) -> SkinConfig:
   607→    """Build a SkinConfig from a raw dict (built-in or loaded from YAML)."""
   608→    # Start with default values as base for missing keys
   609→    default = _BUILTIN_SKINS["default"]
   610→    colors = dict(default.get("colors", {}))
   611→    colors.update(data.get("colors", {}))
   612→    colors_light = dict(default.get("colors_light", {}))
   613→    colors_light.update(data.get("colors_light", {}))
   614→    spinner = dict(default.get("spinner", {}))
   615→    spinner.update(data.get("spinner", {}))
   616→    branding = dict(default.get("branding", {}))
   617→    branding.update(data.get("branding", {}))
   618→
   619→    return SkinConfig(
   620→        name=data.get("name", "unknown"),
   621→        description=data.get("description", ""),
   622→        colors=colors,
   623→        colors_light=colors_light,
   624→        spinner=spinner,
   625→        branding=branding,
   626→        tool_prefix=data.get("tool_prefix", default.get("tool_prefix", "┊")),
   627→        banner_logo=data.get("banner_logo", ""),
   628→        banner_hero=data.get("banner_hero", ""),
   629→    )
   630→
   631→
   632→def list_skins() -> List[Dict[str, str]]:
   633→    """List all available skins (built-in + user-installed).
   634→
   635→    Returns list of {"name": ..., "description": ..., "source": "builtin"|"user"}.
   636→    """
   637→    result = []
   638→    for name, data in _BUILTIN_SKINS.items():
   639→        result.append({
   640→            "name": name,
   641→            "description": data.get("description", ""),
   642→            "source": "builtin",
   643→        })
   644→
   645→    skins_path = _skins_dir()
   646→    if skins_path.is_dir():
   647→        for f in sorted(skins_path.glob("*.yaml")):
   648→            data = _load_skin_from_yaml(f)
   649→            if data:
   650→                skin_name = data.get("name", f.stem)
   651→                # Skip if it shadows a built-in
   652→                if any(s["name"] == skin_name for s in result):
   653→                    continue
   654→                result.append({
   655→                    "name": skin_name,
   656→                    "description": data.get("description", ""),
   657→                    "source": "user",
   658→                })
   659→
   660→    return result
   661→
   662→
   663→def load_skin(name: str) -> SkinConfig:
   664→    """Load a skin by name. Checks user skins first, then built-in."""
   665→    # Check user skins directory
   666→    skins_path = _skins_dir()
   667→    user_file = skins_path / f"{name}.yaml"
   668→    if user_file.is_file():
   669→        data = _load_skin_from_yaml(user_file)
   670→        if data:
   671→            return _build_skin_config(data)
   672→
   673→    # Check built-in skins
   674→    if name in _BUILTIN_SKINS:
   675→        return _build_skin_config(_BUILTIN_SKINS[name])
   676→
   677→    # Fallback to default
   678→    logger.warning("Skin '%s' not found, using default", name)
   679→    return _build_skin_config(_BUILTIN_SKINS["default"])
   680→
   681→
   682→def get_theme_mode() -> str:
   683→    """Get the resolved theme mode ("light" or "dark").
   684→
   685→    If set to "auto", detects the terminal background on first call and caches it.
   686→    Falls back to "dark" if detection fails.
   687→
   688→    When auto-detection succeeds, the result is persisted to
   689→    ``display.detected_background`` in config.yaml so subsequent startups
   690→    can skip the 200ms OSC 11 probe.  The cache is invalidated when the
   691→    user runs ``/skin theme auto`` (which clears the key) or changes
   692→    ``theme_mode`` explicitly.
   693→    """
   694→    global _resolved_theme_mode
   695→    if _theme_mode in ("light", "dark"):
   696→        return _theme_mode
   697→    # Auto-detect
   698→    if _resolved_theme_mode is None:
   699→        try:
   700→            from hermes_cli.colors import detect_terminal_background
   701→            result = detect_terminal_background()
   702→            _resolved_theme_mode = result if result != "unknown" else "dark"
   703→        except Exception:
   704→            _resolved_theme_mode = "dark"
   705→        # Persist to config so next startup skips detection
   706→        try:
   707→            from hermes_cli.config import save_config_value
   708→            save_config_value("display.detected_background", _resolved_theme_mode)
   709→        except Exception:
   710→            pass
   711→    return _resolved_theme_mode
   712→
   713→
   714→def get_theme_mode_setting() -> str:
   715→    """Get the raw theme_mode setting ("auto", "light", or "dark")."""
   716→    return _theme_mode
   717→
   718→
   719→def set_theme_mode(mode: str) -> None:
   720→    """Set the theme mode ("auto", "light", or "dark")."""
   721→    global _theme_mode, _resolved_theme_mode
   722→    if mode not in ("auto", "light", "dark"):
   723→        mode = "auto"
   724→    _theme_mode = mode
   725→    _resolved_theme_mode = None  # Reset cache so auto re-detects
   726→
   727→
   728→def get_active_skin() -> SkinConfig:
   729→    """Get the currently active skin config (cached)."""
   730→    global _active_skin
   731→    if _active_skin is None:
   732→        _active_skin = load_skin(_active_skin_name)
   733→    return _active_skin
   734→
   735→
   736→def set_active_skin(name: str) -> SkinConfig:
   737→    """Switch the active skin. Returns the new SkinConfig."""
   738→    global _active_skin, _active_skin_name
   739→    _active_skin_name = name
   740→    _active_skin = load_skin(name)
   741→    return _active_skin
   742→
   743→
   744→def get_active_skin_name() -> str:
   745→    """Get the name of the currently active skin."""
   746→    return _active_skin_name
   747→
   748→
   749→def init_skin_from_config(config: dict) -> None:
   750→    """Initialize the active skin and theme mode from CLI config at startup.
   751→
   752→    Call this once during CLI init with the loaded config dict.
   753→
   754→    If ``theme_mode`` is ``auto`` and a previous detection result is cached
   755→    in ``display.detected_background``, the cached value is used directly
   756→    so we skip the OSC 11 probe (saves ~200ms on startup).
   757→    """
   758→    global _resolved_theme_mode
   759→
   760→    display = config.get("display", {})
   761→    skin_name = display.get("skin", "default")
   762→    if isinstance(skin_name, str) and skin_name.strip():
   763→        set_active_skin(skin_name.strip())
   764→    else:
   765→        set_active_skin("default")
   766→
   767→    theme_mode = display.get("theme_mode", "auto")
   768→    if isinstance(theme_mode, str) and theme_mode.strip():
   769→        set_theme_mode(theme_mode.strip())
   770→    else:
   771→        set_theme_mode("auto")
   772→
   773→    # Seed resolved mode from cached detection if available
   774→    if _theme_mode == "auto":
   775→        cached = display.get("detected_background")
   776→        if cached in ("light", "dark"):
   777→            _resolved_theme_mode = cached
   778→
```

    90→COMPACT_BANNER = """
    91→[bold #FFD700]╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗[/]
    92→[bold #FFD700]║[/]  [#FFBF00]⚕ NOUS HERMES[/] [dim #B8860B]- AI Agent Framework[/]              [bold #FFD700]║[/]
    93→[bold #FFD700]║[/]  [#CD7F32]Messenger of the Digital Gods[/]    [dim #B8860B]Nous Research[/]   [bold #FFD700]║[/]
    94→[bold #FFD700]╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝[/]
    95→"""
    96→
    97→
    98→# =========================================================================
    99→# Skills scanning
   100→# =========================================================================
   101→
   102→def get_available_skills() -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
   103→    """Scan ~/.hermes/skills/ and return skills grouped by category."""
   104→    import os
   105→
   106→    hermes_home = Path(os.getenv("HERMES_HOME", Path.home() / ".hermes"))
   107→    skills_dir = hermes_home / "skills"
   108→    skills_by_category = {}
   109→
   110→    if not skills_dir.exists():
   111→        return skills_by_category
   112→
   113→    for skill_file in skills_dir.rglob("SKILL.md"):
   114→        rel_path = skill_file.relative_to(skills_dir)
   115→        parts = rel_path.parts
   116→        if len(parts) >= 2:
   117→            category = parts[0]
   118→            skill_name = parts[-2]
   119→        else:
   120→            category = "general"
   121→            skill_name = skill_file.parent.name
   122→        skills_by_category.setdefault(category, []).append(skill_name)
   123→
   124→    return skills_by_category
   125→
   126→
   127→# =========================================================================
   128→# Update check
   129→# =========================================================================
   130→
   131→# Cache update check results for 6 hours to avoid repeated git fetches
   132→_UPDATE_CHECK_CACHE_SECONDS = 6 * 3600
   133→
   134→
   135→def check_for_updates() -> Optional[int]:
   136→    """Check how many commits behind origin/main the local repo is.
   137→
   138→    Does a ``git fetch`` at most once every 6 hours (cached to
   139→    ``~/.hermes/.update_check``).  Returns the number of commits behind,
   140→    or ``None`` if the check fails or isn't applicable.
   141→    """
   142→    hermes_home = Path(os.getenv("HERMES_HOME", Path.home() / ".hermes"))
   143→    repo_dir = hermes_home / "hermes-agent"
   144→    cache_file = hermes_home / ".update_check"
   145→
   146→    # Must be a git repo
   147→    if not (repo_dir / ".git").exists():
   148→        return None
   149→
   150→    # Read cache
   151→    now = time.time()
   152→    try:
   153→        if cache_file.exists():
   154→            cached = json.loads(cache_file.read_text())
   155→            if now - cached.get("ts", 0) < _UPDATE_CHECK_CACHE_SECONDS:
   156→                return cached.get("behind")
   157→    except Exception:
   158→        pass
   159→
   160→    # Fetch latest refs (fast — only downloads ref metadata, no files)
   161→    try:
   162→        subprocess.run(
   163→            ["git", "fetch", "origin", "--quiet"],
   164→            capture_output=True, timeout=10,
   165→            cwd=str(repo_dir),
   166→        )
   167→    except Exception:
   168→        pass  # Offline or timeout — use stale refs, that's fine
   169→
   170→    # Count commits behind
   171→    try:
   172→        result = subprocess.run(
   173→            ["git", "rev-list", "--count", "HEAD..origin/main"],
   174→            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5,
   175→            cwd=str(repo_dir),
   176→        )
   177→        if result.returncode == 0:
   178→            behind = int(result.stdout.strip())
   179→        else:
   180→            behind = None
   181→    except Exception:
   182→        behind = None
   183→
   184→    # Write cache
   185→    try:
   186→        cache_file.write_text(json.dumps({"ts": now, "behind": behind}))
   187→    except Exception:
   188→        pass
   189→
   190→    return behind
   191→
   192→
   193→# =========================================================================
   194→# Welcome banner
   195→# =========================================================================
   196→
   197→def _format_context_length(tokens: int) -> str:
   198→    """Format a token count for display (e.g. 128000 → '128K', 1048576 → '1M')."""
   199→    if tokens >= 1_000_000:
   200→        val = tokens / 1_000_000
   201→        return f"{val:g}M"
   202→    elif tokens >= 1_000:
   203→        val = tokens / 1_000
   204→        return f"{val:g}K"
   205→    return str(tokens)
   206→
   207→
   208→def build_welcome_banner(console: Console, model: str, cwd: str,
   209→                         tools: List[dict] = None,
   210→                         enabled_toolsets: List[str] = None,
   211→                         session_id: str = None,
   212→                         get_toolset_for_tool=None,
   213→                         context_length: int = None):
   214→    """Build and print a welcome banner with caduceus on left and info on right.
   215→
   216→    Args:
   217→        console: Rich Console instance.
   218→        model: Current model name.
   219→        cwd: Current working directory.
   220→        tools: List of tool definitions.
   221→        enabled_toolsets: List of enabled toolset names.
   222→        session_id: Session identifier.
   223→        get_toolset_for_tool: Callable to map tool name -> toolset name.
   224→        context_length: Model's context window size in tokens.
   225→    """
   226→    from model_tools import check_tool_availability, TOOLSET_REQUIREMENTS
   227→    if get_toolset_for_tool is None:
   228→        from model_tools import get_toolset_for_tool
   229→
   230→    tools = tools or []
   231→    enabled_toolsets = enabled_toolsets or []
   232→
   233→    _, unavailable_toolsets = check_tool_availability(quiet=True)
   234→    disabled_tools = set()
   235→    for item in unavailable_toolsets:
   236→        disabled_tools.update(item.get("tools", []))
   237→
   238→    layout_table = Table.grid(padding=(0, 2))
   239→    layout_table.add_column("left", justify="center")
   240→    layout_table.add_column("right", justify="left")
   241→
   242→    # Resolve skin colors once for the entire banner
   243→    accent = _skin_color("banner_accent", "#FFBF00")
   244→    dim = _skin_color("banner_dim", "#B8860B")
   245→    text = _skin_color("banner_text", "#FFF8DC")
   246→    session_color = _skin_color("session_border", "#8B8682")
   247→
   248→    left_lines = ["", HERMES_CADUCEUS, ""]
   249→    model_short = model.split("/")[-1] if "/" in model else model
   250→    if len(model_short) > 28:
   251→        model_short = model_short[:25] + "..."
   252→    ctx_str = f" [dim {dim}]·[/] [dim {dim}]{_format_context_length(context_length)} context[/]" if context_length else ""
   253→    left_lines.append(f"[{accent}]{model_short}[/]{ctx_str} [dim {dim}]·[/] [dim {dim}]Nous Research[/]")
   254→    left_lines.append(f"[dim {dim}]{cwd}[/]")
   255→    if session_id:
   256→        left_lines.append(f"[dim {session_color}]Session: {session_id}[/]")
   257→    left_content = "\n".join(left_lines)
   258→
   259→    right_lines = [f"[bold {accent}]Available Tools[/]"]
   260→    toolsets_dict: Dict[str, list] = {}
   261→
   262→    for tool in tools:
   263→        tool_name = tool["function"]["name"]
   264→        toolset = get_toolset_for_tool(tool_name) or "other"
   265→        toolsets_dict.setdefault(toolset, []).append(tool_name)
   266→
   267→    for item in unavailable_toolsets:
   268→        toolset_id = item.get("id", item.get("name", "unknown"))
   269→        display_name = f"{toolset_id}_tools" if not toolset_id.endswith("_tools") else toolset_id
   270→        if display_name not in toolsets_dict:
   271→            toolsets_dict[display_name] = []
   272→        for tool_name in item.get("tools", []):
   273→            if tool_name not in toolsets_dict[display_name]:
   274→                toolsets_dict[display_name].append(tool_name)
   275→
   276→    sorted_toolsets = sorted(toolsets_dict.keys())
   277→    display_toolsets = sorted_toolsets[:8]
   278→    remaining_toolsets = len(sorted_toolsets) - 8
   279→
   280→    for toolset in display_toolsets:
   281→        tool_names = toolsets_dict[toolset]
   282→        colored_names = []
   283→        for name in sorted(tool_names):
   284→            if name in disabled_tools:
   285→                colored_names.append(f"[red]{name}[/]")
   286→            else:
   287→                colored_names.append(f"[{text}]{name}[/]")
   288→
   289→        tools_str = ", ".join(colored_names)
   290→        if len(", ".join(sorted(tool_names))) > 45:
   291→            short_names = []
   292→            length = 0
   293→            for name in sorted(tool_names):
   294→                if length + len(name) + 2 > 42:
   295→                    short_names.append("...")
   296→                    break
   297→                short_names.append(name)
   298→                length += len(name) + 2
   299→            colored_names = []
   300→            for name in short_names:
   301→                if name == "...":
   302→                    colored_names.append("[dim]...[/]")
   303→                elif name in disabled_tools:
   304→                    colored_names.append(f"[red]{name}[/]")
   305→                else:
   306→                    colored_names.append(f"[{text}]{name}[/]")
   307→            tools_str = ", ".join(colored_names)
   308→
   309→        right_lines.append(f"[dim #B8860B]{toolset}:[/] {tools_str}")
   310→
   311→    if remaining_toolsets > 0:
   312→        right_lines.append(f"[dim #B8860B](and {remaining_toolsets} more toolsets...)[/]")
   313→
   314→    # MCP Servers section (only if configured)
   315→    try:
   316→        from tools.mcp_tool import get_mcp_status
   317→        mcp_status = get_mcp_status()
   318→    except Exception:
   319→        mcp_status = []
   320→
   321→    if mcp_status:
   322→        right_lines.append("")
   323→        right_lines.append("[bold #FFBF00]MCP Servers[/]")
   324→        for srv in mcp_status:
   325→            if srv["connected"]:
   326→                right_lines.append(
   327→                    f"[dim #B8860B]{srv['name']}[/] [#FFF8DC]({srv['transport']})[/] "
   328→                    f"[dim #B8860B]—[/] [#FFF8DC]{srv['tools']} tool(s)[/]"
   329→                )
   330→            else:
   331→                right_lines.append(
   332→                    f"[red]{srv['name']}[/] [dim]({srv['transport']})[/] "
   333→                    f"[red]— failed[/]"
   334→                )
   335→
   336→    right_lines.append("")
   337→    right_lines.append(f"[bold {accent}]Available Skills[/]")
   338→    skills_by_category = get_available_skills()
   339→    total_skills = sum(len(s) for s in skills_by_category.values())
   340→
   341→    if skills_by_category:
   342→        for category in sorted(skills_by_category.keys()):
   343→            skill_names = sorted(skills_by_category[category])
   344→            if len(skill_names) > 8:
   345→                display_names = skill_names[:8]
   346→                skills_str = ", ".join(display_names) + f" +{len(skill_names) - 8} more"
   347→            else:
   348→                skills_str = ", ".join(skill_names)
   349→            if len(skills_str) > 50:
   350→                skills_str = skills_str[:47] + "..."
   351→            right_lines.append(f"[dim {dim}]{category}:[/] [{text}]{skills_str}[/]")
   352→    else:
   353→        right_lines.append(f"[dim {dim}]No skills installed[/]")
   354→
   355→    right_lines.append("")
   356→    mcp_connected = sum(1 for s in mcp_status if s["connected"]) if mcp_status else 0
   357→    summary_parts = [f"{len(tools)} tools", f"{total_skills} skills"]
   358→    if mcp_connected:
   359→        summary_parts.append(f"{mcp_connected} MCP servers")
   360→    summary_parts.append("/help for commands")
   361→    right_lines.append(f"[dim {dim}]{' · '.join(summary_parts)}[/]")
   362→
   363→    # Update check — show if behind origin/main
   364→    try:
   365→        behind = check_for_updates()
   366→        if behind and behind > 0:
   367→            commits_word = "commit" if behind == 1 else "commits"
   368→            right_lines.append(
   369→                f"[bold yellow]⚠ {behind} {commits_word} behind[/]"
   370→                f"[dim yellow] — run [bold]hermes update[/bold] to update[/]"
   371→            )
   372→    except Exception:
   373→        pass  # Never break the banner over an update check
   374→
   375→    right_content = "\n".join(right_lines)
   376→    layout_table.add_row(left_content, right_content)
   377→
   378→    agent_name = _skin_branding("agent_name", "Hermes Agent")
   379→    title_color = _skin_color("banner_title", "#FFD700")
   380→    border_color = _skin_color("banner_border", "#CD7F32")
   381→    outer_panel = Panel(
   382→        layout_table,
   383→        title=f"[bold {title_color}]{agent_name} v{VERSION} ({RELEASE_DATE})[/]",
   384→        border_style=border_color,
   385→        padding=(0, 2),
   386→    )
   387→
   388→    console.print()
   389→    console.print(HERMES_AGENT_LOGO)
   390→    console.print()
   391→    console.print(outer_panel)
   392→
```

     8→    hermes gateway             # Run gateway in foreground
     9→    hermes gateway start       # Start gateway as service
    10→    hermes gateway stop        # Stop gateway service
    11→    hermes gateway status      # Show gateway status
    12→    hermes gateway install     # Install gateway service
    13→    hermes gateway uninstall   # Uninstall gateway service
    14→    hermes setup               # Interactive setup wizard
    15→    hermes logout              # Clear stored authentication
    16→    hermes status              # Show status of all components
    17→    hermes cron                # Manage cron jobs
    18→    hermes cron list           # List cron jobs
    19→    hermes cron status         # Check if cron scheduler is running
    20→    hermes doctor              # Check configuration and dependencies
    21→    hermes honcho setup                    # Configure Honcho AI memory integration
    22→    hermes honcho status                   # Show Honcho config and connection status
    23→    hermes honcho sessions                 # List directory → session name mappings
    24→    hermes honcho map <name>               # Map current directory to a session name
    25→    hermes honcho peer                     # Show peer names and dialectic settings
    26→    hermes honcho peer --user NAME         # Set user peer name
    27→    hermes honcho peer --ai NAME           # Set AI peer name
    28→    hermes honcho peer --reasoning LEVEL   # Set dialectic reasoning level
    29→    hermes honcho mode                     # Show current memory mode
    30→    hermes honcho mode [hybrid|honcho|local]  # Set memory mode
    31→    hermes honcho tokens                   # Show token budget settings
    32→    hermes honcho tokens --context N       # Set session.context() token cap
    33→    hermes honcho tokens --dialectic N     # Set dialectic result char cap
    34→    hermes honcho identity                 # Show AI peer identity representation
    35→    hermes honcho identity <file>          # Seed AI peer identity from a file (SOUL.md etc.)
    36→    hermes honcho migrate                  # Step-by-step migration guide: OpenClaw native → Hermes + Honcho
    37→    hermes version             # Show version
    38→    hermes update              # Update to latest version
    39→    hermes uninstall           # Uninstall Hermes Agent
    40→    hermes sessions browse     # Interactive session picker with search
    41→    hermes claw migrate        # Migrate from OpenClaw to Hermes
    42→    hermes claw migrate --dry-run  # Preview migration without changes
    43→"""
    44→
    45→import argparse
    46→import os
    47→import sys
    48→from pathlib import Path
    49→from typing import Optional
    50→
    51→# Add project root to path
    52→PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).parent.parent.resolve()
    53→sys.path.insert(0, str(PROJECT_ROOT))
    54→
    55→# Load .env from ~/.hermes/.env first, then project root as dev fallback
    56→from dotenv import load_dotenv
    57→from hermes_cli.config import get_env_path, get_hermes_home
    58→_user_env = get_env_path()
    59→if _user_env.exists():
    60→    try:
    61→        load_dotenv(dotenv_path=_user_env, encoding="utf-8")
    62→    except UnicodeDecodeError:
    63→        load_dotenv(dotenv_path=_user_env, encoding="latin-1")
    64→load_dotenv(dotenv_path=PROJECT_ROOT / '.env', override=False)
    65→
    66→# Point mini-swe-agent at ~/.hermes/ so it shares our config
    67→os.environ.setdefault("MSWEA_GLOBAL_CONFIG_DIR", str(get_hermes_home()))
    68→os.environ.setdefault("MSWEA_SILENT_STARTUP", "1")
    69→
    70→import logging
    71→
    72→from hermes_cli import __version__, __release_date__
    73→from hermes_constants import OPENROUTER_BASE_URL
    74→
    75→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

    77→
    78→def _has_any_provider_configured() -> bool:
    79→    """Check if at least one inference provider is usable."""

    83→    # Check env vars (may be set by .env or shell).
    84→    # OPENAI_BASE_URL alone counts — local models (vLLM, llama.cpp, etc.)
    85→    # often don't require an API key.

    88→    # Collect all provider env vars
    89→    provider_env_vars = {"OPENROUTER_API_KEY", "OPENAI_API_KEY", "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "ANTHROPIC_TOKEN", "OPENAI_BASE_URL"}
    90→    for pconfig in PROVIDER_REGISTRY.values():
    91→        if pconfig.auth_type == "api_key":
    92→            provider_env_vars.update(pconfig.api_key_env_vars)
    93→    if any(os.getenv(v) for v in provider_env_vars):
    94→        return True
    95→
    96→    # Check .env file for keys
    97→    env_file = get_env_path()
    98→    if env_file.exists():
    99→        try:
   100→            for line in env_file.read_text().splitlines():

## Comprehensive Color Usage Report

I've completed a thorough search of the codebase for all color-related terminal output. Here's the complete inventory:

### 1. **Colors Module** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/hermes_cli/colors.py`)
Primary ANSI color definitions using escape codes:
- `\033[0m` - RESET
- `\033[1m` - BOLD
- `\033[2m` - DIM
- `\033[31m` - RED
- `\033[32m` - GREEN
- `\033[33m` - YELLOW
- `\033[34m` - BLUE
- `\033[35m` - MAGENTA
- `\033[36m` - CYAN

Also includes `detect_terminal_background()` function that uses OSC 11 escape sequence to detect light/dark terminal background.

### 2. **Skin Engine** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/hermes_cli/skin_engine.py`)
Central color configuration system with 6 built-in skins, each with hex colors for:

**Default Skin Colors:**
- `banner_border: #CD7F32` (bronze)
- `banner_title: #FFD700` (gold)
- `banner_accent: #FFBF00` (light gold)
- `banner_dim: #B8860B` (dark gold)
- `banner_text: #FFF8DC` (cornsilk)
- `ui_accent: #FFBF00`
- `ui_label: #4dd0e1` (cyan)
- `ui_ok: #4caf50` (green)
- `ui_error: #ef5350` (red)
- `ui_warn: #ffa726` (orange)
- `prompt: #FFF8DC`
- `input_rule: #CD7F32`
- `response_border: #FFD700`
- `session_label: #DAA520` (goldenrod)
- `session_border: #8B8682` (gray)

**Light Mode Overrides:**
- `banner_title: #B8860B`
- `banner_accent: #996600`
- `banner_text: #5C4A00`
- `prompt: #5C4A00`
- `ui_accent: #996600`
- `ui_label: #0277BD`
- `response_border: #B8860B`
- `session_label: #8B6914`

**Other Built-in Skins with Hex Colors:**
1. **Ares** (war theme): #9F1C1C, #C7A96B, #DD4A3A, #6B1717, #F1E6CF (crimson/bronze)
2. **Mono** (grayscale): #555555, #e6edf3, #aaaaaa, #444444, #c9d1d9, #888888
3. **Slate** (blue): #4169e1, #7eb8f6, #8EA8FF, #4b5563, #c9d1d9, #63D0A6, #F7A072, #e6a855
4. **Poseidon** (ocean): #2A6FB9, #A9DFFF, #5DB8F5, #153C73, #EAF7FF, #496884
5. **Sisyphus** (austere grayscale): #B7B7B7, #F5F5F5, #E7E7E7, #4A4A4A, #D3D3D3, #919191, #656565
6. **Charizard** (volcanic): #C75B1D, #FFD39A, #F29C38, #7A3511, #FFF0D4, #6C4724, #B85A1D, #8B4513

Each skin supports custom `colors_light` overrides for light mode.

### 3. **Banner Module** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/hermes_cli/banner.py`)
Rich markup colors in ASCII art and welcome banners:
- Logo: Uses `[bold #FFD700]`, `[#FFBF00]`, `[#CD7F32]`
- Caduceus: Uses `[#CD7F32]`, `[#FFBF00]`, `[#FFD700]`, `[#B8860B]`
- Compact banner: `[bold #FFD700]`, `[#FFBF00]`, `[dim #B8860B]`

Skin-aware colors pulled from active skin via `_skin_color()` and `_skin_branding()` functions.

### 4. **CLI Main** (`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/hermes-agent/cli.py`)
